### Every job in full

#### KIT-NORA-HAND · SH01

- **job type**: image
- **method**: Practical reference capture at the wardrobe fitting, with the contracted hand double, once the mole and scar are built and checked. Not generated. Outputs CHR-NORA-HAND@v1 and LOOK-NORA-L01@v1. Attached to SH01, its first use. It is the check reference for SH01, SH02A, SH02B, SH04, SH05 and SH07.
- **prompt**: Reference set of the hand double's hands and forearms in the felted ink-blue house pullover, laid on a neutral grey card beside a centimetre ruler and an 18% grey card, soft even light from camera-left, pores and knuckle creases sharp. Views: the back of the right hand flat, with the built mole; the back of the left hand flat, with the built scar across the index finger's middle joint; the left hand closed on a dowel the size of the kettle handle; both palms; both forearms flat at rest, then reaching forward, with the cuff edge and the wrist bone in every forearm view. Skin matte, nails short and bare, fingers and wrists bare.
- **acceptance checks**: The mole (4 mm, 2 cm wristward of the middle knuckle) reads on the back of the right hand only. The scar (10 mm, across the index finger's middle joint) reads on the back of the left hand only. If either shows on the other hand, the file is mirrored: reject.; The cuff sits 7 cm above the wrist bone at rest and 9 cm reaching, never under 5 cm, on both arms. There is nothing on the fingers or wrists, and the skin is matte.; Skin value, hand shape (8.5 cm across the knuckles, middle finger about 7 cm) and nails are logged against the ruler and the 18% card, so they can be matched on every hand plate.; The double's signed release covers this capture before it is shot (see the release blocker).
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: No generator. Every hand plate uses this same double, so this set carries the mirror and continuity checks. It can also serve as the style bible's fitting photo of both built marks beside the ruler.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; fitting time is the producer's

#### KIT-PROP01-SAMPLE · SH02A

- **job type**: image
- **method**: Practical reference capture of the brand-confirmed real sample (client photos or a studio packshot). Not generated. Outputs REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1, views V1-V8, each citable alone (for example REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1:V4). Attached to SH02A, its first use, because SH01 frames the rack out. It feeds SH02A to SH08.
- **prompt**: Reference set of the real production dripper on a neutral grey card, beside a centimetre ruler and an 18% grey card, soft even light from camera-left, sharp from rim to foot, surfaces untouched. The front faces the lens unless stated (the front is the thickest glaze arc at the foot, or the side the brand names if there is none). V1: fifteen degrees down (SH08). V2: thirty-seven degrees down (SH04 to SH06). V3: forty degrees down, with the inside of the empty cone showing (SH02B, SH03). V4: forty-three degrees down, front turned thirty degrees off the lens (SH02A). V5: fifty-three degrees down (SH07). V6: underside and foot. V7: seated on the chipped white mug. V8: lifted in the hand double's right-hand front-rim pinch, index and middle fingers over the rim and thumb on the outer rim band.
- **acceptance checks**: The ruler reads in every view, and the rim, height and foot-band diameters are logged in cm. A rim of 10.5 cm or more keeps glaze showing on both sides of the double's 8.5 cm knuckles; below that, casting re-checks the hand. If the foot band is not the assumed 10.0/8.4 cm, the SH02B seat is re-planned.; Each view is labelled V1-V8 with its angle and front bearing, and colour is logged against the 18% card for SH08's neutral match.; Brand sign-off lists which of the assumed features the real unit has: handle-less cone, cream satin glaze, speck, gloss rim band, uneven foot pool, raw foot band. No SH02A-SH08 plate is shot before the sign-off.; The exterior is unmarked, as made. Any maker's mark is on the underside only.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: No generator. Product views come only from the real sample, CAD or a shoot. This file does not exist yet (CLAIM), and every product frame waits on it. V8 uses the hand double, so it needs the release.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; sample and packshot cost is the producer's

#### KIT-PROP02-GLOW · SH05

- **job type**: image
- **method**: Pre-shoot practical glow test on the built set, at SH05's lock and exposure, using the re-specced case. Not generated. Outputs REF-PROP02-GLOW@v1, the state reference that PASS-SH05-GLOW@v1 and SH05-COMP must match. It can run in the same prelight as the camera plan's ring test.
- **prompt**: Stills from SH05's locked position at its exposure, the room lit as for the take: the face-down phone in its stone-grey case, riding on its four corner pads at the settled mark, touching the white mug of coffee on the oiled oak, cable plugged in. Its screen is lit underneath to a full bright field. Where light escapes the open edges, a thin cool spill lies on the oak, and the gap shows only soft, even light. One matching frame with the screen off. The frame holds only the counter set, and everything is still.
- **acceptance checks**: For each edge, the test logs whether light leaks, how far it reaches on the oak in mm (the bible assumes 5-15 mm) and its level in stops over the lit oak, all at SH05's exposure.; Nothing on the screen is legible through any gap, and no light falls on the mug.; It is shot on the case built to the bible: four corner pads 1.5 mm proud, open along all four edges. A case without the gap fails.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: No generator, and no product reference is needed. If no edge leaks, SH05-COMP adds no glow and the rings carry the beat, since the storyboard rates the glow a secondary cue.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; prelight time is the producer's

#### SH01-PLT · SH01

- **job type**: video
- **method**: Practical plate with the hand double, locked off on a high arm. The rigged phone shows a blank lit field, buzzes until the hand lands, and cuts to dark by the landing frame. Outputs PLT-SH01@v1, which feeds SH01-COMP.
- **prompt**: Steep view down onto the oak counter from above and behind the right shoulder, window light from frame-left three-quarter back, soft fill from frame-right. A face-up phone in a stone-grey case lies as a diamond left of centre, its screen an evenly lit blank light-grey field, the brightest thing in frame, buzzing in place. Its cable loops out frame-left and runs along the back of the counter at upper left. The empty white mug stands right of it, a pale tide line inside. At 0.58 seconds a right hand enters from the bottom edge left of centre, lit from below by the screen, and lands at 0.8 seconds without hovering: thumb on the phone's frame-left long edge, fingertips on its mug-side edge. The buzzing stops. The hand lifts the phone two centimetres, rolls it face-down toward the mug and lets go a centimetre up. The case knocks down against the mug at 1.2 seconds and is dark from that frame. The hand carries on frame-right past the mug, still moving at the cut at 1.5 seconds. Phone glass and mug rim are sharp, the forearm soft. Only the hand, the forearm and a hand's width of sleeve show. Ambient kitchen room tone only.
- **reference asset ids**: CHR-NORA-HAND@v1; LOOK-NORA-L01@v1
- **acceptance checks**: From 0 to 1.2 s the field is blank and evenly lit, with no clock, date, notification or status text from the handset. It is dark from the landing frame at 1.2 s, and after that there is no light on the oak around the case.; The phone buzzes from 0 to 0.8 s and stops as the hand lands, with nothing vibrating in the hand. The hand lands without hovering and rolls the phone in one move. The cable stays in the port, and its run along the back is unmoved.; At the release, a centimetre above the oak, no finger or thumb is between the phone and the mug. The phone falls the last centimetre on its own, and its edge knocks the mug.; No elbow or upper arm enters at bottom-left. Only 5-15 cm of sleeve shows, one flat ink-blue. The mole is on the back of the right hand only.; Continuity: the phone ends face-down and dark, touching the mug. The mug, its handle and its chip are unmoved, and the rack stays out frame-right. The hand is moving frame-right at the cut, and SH02A picks it up entering frame-left.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Practical, as boarded. Triage 7: screens 2, light switching 2 (the screen cuts at landing), reflections 1 (phone glass, mug glaze), fast motion 1 (habit-speed roll), cloth 1 (cuff). The phone is a prop, so there is no product contact. The alternatives (practical plus composite) are already in use. If the rigged cut misses the landing frame, SH01-COMP paints out the leftover glow. Compound action flagged (land, lift, roll, release) but kept as one take. The board now sets the grip and the 1 cm release (19-29f), which closes last round's director blocker.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; shoot cost is the producer's

#### SH01-COMP · SH01

- **job type**: video
- **method**: Composite: ART-LOCK-0700@v1 tracked onto PLT-SH01@v1's blank field, applied after any flop. Nothing is generated. Art spec of record, now shared with the camera plan: '7:00' at 2.4 cm cap height on the glass, #1B1F24 on #E4E7EA, centred in the upper half of the screen, upright to the phone with its top toward the phone's top edge. Below it sits one generic banner: a rounded #FFFFFF bar with a blank #B8BDC3 icon square and two #8A9097 lines too small to read, with no glyphs. No date and no status bar. The typeface is a generic grotesque, not any handset maker's lock-screen typeface or layout. Size estimate: SH01's frame is 34 cm wide (65mm at 1.1 m on a 20.25 mm gate), so 1080 ÷ 34 ≈ 32 px per cm. That puts 2.4 cm at about 76 px flat and about 65 px on screen after roughly 0.85 foreshortening; the camera plan gives 65-75 px. The measured height is the pass mark. The landing fingers are rotoscoped over the art, and any leftover glow after 1.2 s is painted out. Outputs COMP-SH01@v1.
- **prompt**: The phone's lit field carries only the supplied art: the time, 7:00, in dark plain digits centred in the upper half of the screen, and below it one pale rounded banner holding a blank grey square and two grey lines too small to read. Both stay upright to the phone, leaning about 27 degrees frame-right with the handset. They stay pinned to the glass while it buzzes and read at phone size through the first 0.8 seconds. As the hand rolls the phone, the fingers pass in front of the art and it turns away with the glass. From the landing at 1.2 seconds the screen is dark, and the oak around the case matches the unlit plate. The frame holds only the right hand, the forearm and a little sleeve. Ambient kitchen room tone only.
- **reference asset ids**: PLT-SH01@v1; ART-LOCK-0700@v1
- **acceptance checks**: Measured along the digits, 7:00 is at least 45 px tall in the 1080-wide frame (about 65 px planned), centred near 39% width and 48% height. It reads at 100% on a 6-inch phone within the first 0.8 s, leaning with the phone about 27 degrees frame-right.; The banner carries no legible glyph. There is no date, status bar or extra icon, and no OS trade dress is imitated: no shipping handset's lock-screen typeface, clock layout, wallpaper, lock icon or notification style.; The art tracks the glass through the buzz and the roll with no slip. The landing fingers stay in front of it with clean roto edges, and there is no light on the oak after 1.2 s.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Post composite of designed art. The digits and the banner are never generated, because generated text garbles or picks up a date, a status bar or legible banner glyphs. Triage as SH01-PLT.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; design and compositing time are the producer's

#### SH02A-PLT · SH02A

- **job type**: video
- **method**: Practical plate with the real unit and the hand double, locked off, 24f (1.0 s) as boarded. Outputs PLT-SH02A@v1.
- **prompt**: Looking down over the near wires of a white wire dish rack, window light from frame-left behind, the white plate held a stop darker than the dripper. The dripper stands upright in the rack just below mid-frame, the brightest shape, its front turned a little off the lens, beside the plate and an upturned tumbler. From the first frame a right hand eases in from frame-left, moving right, and hooks the near rim with its index and middle fingers by 0.25 seconds. As the grip closes, the thumb lands at the foot on the lens side and sweeps up across the glaze to the rim band without stopping, by 0.58 seconds. The hand lifts the dripper fourteen centimetres with no turn in the hand, and the foot clears the wires at about 0.9 seconds. From 0.88 seconds the path bends frame-left and away, still moving at the cut at 1.0 second. Thumb, rim and front are sharp, the front wires soft. Only the right hand, the forearm and a little sleeve enter. Ambient kitchen room tone only.
- **reference asset ids**: REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1; CHR-NORA-HAND@v1; LOOK-NORA-L01@v1
- **acceptance checks**: The index and middle fingers hook the near rim by 0.25 s. The thumb sweeps from the foot across the front's glaze to the rim band in one pass, with no stop, by 0.58 s, and it reads as a grip, not a caress. Silhouette and surface match REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1:V4, with no added handle or mark and no turn in the hand. If the signed-off unit has no pool, the thumb crosses the production designer's replacement detail instead.; The foot clears the wires at about 0.9 s, and no wire passes through a finger or the foot. The far rim peaks at about 29% height. The card zone (16-26% height, 10-86% width) holds no hand, dripper or rack part for the whole take.; The plate reads darker than the dripper. Her torso and the faucet stay out, and the sink corner at bottom-right is dry and empty.; Continuity: the empty right hand enters frame-left moving right, answering SH01's frame-right exit. The plate and tumbler are unmoved. At the cut the dripper is 14 cm up, moving frame-left and away, with its front unturned. The mole is on the right hand only.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Practical. Triage 5: hand-on-product 2 (hook, thumb pass, lift), fast motion 1 (lift peak 0.55 m/s or less), cloth 1 (cuff), reflections 1 (gloss rim band, pool). Compound action flagged (hook, thumb pass, lift, set-off, all in 1.0 s), kept as one take and not split. A simpler-action alternative for the director if takes fail: hook and lift without the thumb pass, which loses the in-use 'hand-glazed' read. Pickup trigger: if no take in SH02A's slot passes the thumb-pass, wire-clear and no-turn checks, the director calls a pickup on the real unit with the double. Nothing is generated.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; shoot and any pickup cost are the producer's

#### SH02B-PLT · SH02B

- **job type**: video
- **method**: Practical insert with the real unit and the hand double, locked off, 48f (2.0 s). The shot has no filter action: the filter goes in during the SH02B/SH03 cut. Outputs PLT-SH02B@v1.
- **prompt**: Looking down from just behind the right shoulder onto the white mug at centre, window light from frame-left three-quarter back, soft fill from frame-right. From the first frame the right hand carries the dripper, pinched at its front rim from the lens side, out of the right third toward the mug, twelve centimetres over the oak, easing over the mug by 0.42 seconds. It drops the dripper two and a half centimetres onto the rim, and by 0.58 seconds it is seated, centred, front square to the lens. The fingers unhook, the thumb comes off and the hand rises three centimetres by 0.79 seconds. It then withdraws along the same line and leaves frame-right by 1.46 seconds, clear of the face-down phone at left. The empty dripper sits on the mug, and nothing moves to 2.0 seconds. Rim, foot and phone edge are sharp, the counter front edge soft. Only the right hand and forearm enter, from the bottom edge. Ambient kitchen room tone only.
- **reference asset ids**: REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1; CHR-NORA-HAND@v1; LOOK-NORA-L01@v1
- **acceptance checks**: There is one 2.5 cm drop onto the rim, seated at 0.58 s, centred, with the front square to the lens and the mug's chip unmoved: no skipped or doubled drop, and the foot not inside the rim. Silhouette matches REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1:V3 and V7, and the cone is empty with no filter.; The hand never passes over, touches or nudges the phone, which stays face-down and dark against the mug. The cable leaves frame-left, unmoved.; The card zone (16-26% height, 10-86% width) holds no hand or dripper, and the hand's highest point stays at about 35% height or lower. No outlet or cable shows in that band.; Continuity: the dripper enters near 74% width moving right to left, as SH02A leaves frame-left and away. Nothing moves from 1.46 s to 2.0 s. The mole is on the right hand only.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Practical insert. Triage 5: hand-on-product 2 (carry, seat, release), fast motion 1 (withdrawal peak 0.6 m/s or less), cloth 1 (cuff), reflections 1 (gloss rim band, mug). Compound action flagged (carry, drop, release, rise, withdraw, all in 1.46 s), kept as one take and not split. Timed to the board's click at 14f (0.58 s) after a 12 cm carry, so the review note's 'seat at 0.3 s' no longer applies.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; shoot cost is the producer's

#### SH03-PLT · SH03

- **job type**: video
- **method**: Practical plate at 100% speed with the real unit, real water and real grounds, locked off, 60f (2.5 s). The double pours left-handed off frame. No speed ramp, and no added foam or steam. Outputs PLT-SH03@v1.
- **prompt**: On the same line as the previous shot, two sizes closer, forty degrees down into the dripper's open top on the white mug, the cone centred, window light from frame-left three-quarter back, the dry paper collar the brightest edge. The dry heap sits alone and still. At 0.21 seconds a plain terracotta spout enters from frame-left and stops over the peak. At 0.42 seconds it tips, and one thin stream circles twice, from the centre out to a finger's width short of the paper and back, while the grounds darken, swell and dome. At 2.0 seconds it tips back, the stream stops clean, and the spout lifts out frame-left by 2.2 seconds. The bloom fizzes alone to 2.5 seconds, all at real speed. The phone's dark face-down end sits soft at lower-left. Near rim, stream, dome and far collar are sharp; the mug body and the wall are soft. Only the spout enters, from frame-left. Ambient kitchen room tone only.
- **reference asset ids**: REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1
- **acceptance checks**: It plays at 100% speed with no ramp (taste notes: no slow motion). The stream, steam and foam are only what the real pour made, with nothing added, and the producer's claims check signs off the selected take.; One thin stream runs for both circuits and stops a finger's width short of the paper, and the collar stays dry. The spout is plain, with no mark. No hand, kettle body or scorch band enters frame-left.; Continuity: the cone holds 50% width across the cut-in from SH02B. At entry the heap is dry, about 3 cm below the rim, with the collar 1 cm above it. At exit the grounds are a wet dome about 1 cm high, peaking about 2 cm below the rim, and the paper is dark to 1 cm below the rim. The phone end stays dark at lower-left.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Practical. Triage 3: liquids 2, reflections 1 (wet bloom, gloss rim band). The kettle is a prop, and no hand is in frame, so there is no mirror check. It stays practical because a generated stream or bloom would overstate what the product does. The missing kettle body and scorch band are the script's declared break, and SH07 carries them.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; shoot cost is the producer's

#### SH04-PLT · SH04

- **job type**: video
- **method**: Practical plate: one continuous locked take from SH04 into SH05, with the real unit and the hand double. This job covers SH04's 36f (1.5 s) and outputs PLT-SH04@v1; the same take continues as PLT-SH05@v1.
- **prompt**: The widest counter frame, from the aisle side, window light raking from frame-left, soft fill from the ceiling over the aisle, the dripper the brightest shape. The right hand's fingers claw over the dripper's rim, palm clear of the spent paper, and lift it three centimetres straight up off the white mug, holding it level until the last drop falls into the coffee at 0.17 seconds. From 0.25 seconds it moves in one arc frame-right until the foot clears the mug, then down onto its mark on the bare oak, on the mug's far side from the phone. It lands by 0.83 seconds with its front still square to the lens. The fingertips open off the glaze, and the hand rises two centimetres by 1.04 seconds. Then it returns to the mug, and by 1.5 seconds the fingertips rest along its side below the rim, palm clear, heel on the oak. The face-down phone stays dark against the mug at left. The forearm leaves frame-left, with the cuff at the edge. Ambient kitchen room tone only.
- **reference asset ids**: REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1; CHR-NORA-HAND@v1; LOOK-NORA-L01@v1
- **acceptance checks**: The claw grip holds over the rim, palm clear of the spent collar, from the lift until the release after the 0.83 s landing. There is no turn in the hand and no drip on the outer glaze. The foot lands within 1 cm of the set-down mark on clean oak, with no ring, stain or drip.; The last drop falls into the coffee within 0.17-0.42 s, not onto the oak, and the coffee holds at the tide line.; Continuity: at entry the dripper sits on the mug with the spent filter in. At exit it stands released on the mark. The heel is on the oak by 1.38 s, and by 1.5 s the fingertips lie along the mug's side below the rim, palm clear, with the handle, the chip and the phone contact clear. The take runs on into SH05 with no cut.; The rack stays out frame-right, no window jamb shows at frame-left, and her torso stays out. The mole is on the right hand only, and the cuff is one flat ink-blue with no ribbing.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Practical. Triage 5: hand-on-product 2 (lift, hold, arc, set down within 1 cm), liquids 1 (last drop), cloth 1 (cuff), reflections 1 (gloss rim, mug, coffee). No product rotation. Compound action flagged (lift, hold for the drop, arc, set down, release, return to the mug, all in 1.5 s), kept as one take with SH05 and not split. A late drop shifts everything after it, and SH05's tail absorbs the shift.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; shoot cost is the producer's

#### SH05-PLT · SH05

- **job type**: video
- **method**: Practical plate: the same continuous take after SH04, SH05's 72f (3.0 s), with the phone rigged to rattle and its screen dark. Outputs PLT-SH05@v1. Then a clean pass at the same lock and exposure: screen lit face-down, phone still, nothing else moving. Outputs PASS-SH05-GLOW@v1, which feeds SH05-COMP.
- **prompt**: Same locked wide, window light raking from frame-left. The right hand's fingertips rest along the white mug's side below the rim, palm clear, heel on the oak, and the whole hand, thumb included, stays still for all three seconds. At 0.21 seconds the face-down phone touching the mug buzzes and rattles a few millimetres against it. Fine rings cross the coffee under the fingertips, bending the pale reflected line in its surface. At 1.0 second the rattle stops and the phone settles back against the mug. The coffee is flat again by about 1.25 seconds, and the hand holds to the end. The dripper stands on its mark at right throughout, and the bare oak below it stays empty. The forearm leaves frame-left, with the cuff at the edge. Ambient kitchen room tone only.
- **reference asset ids**: REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1; CHR-NORA-HAND@v1; LOOK-NORA-L01@v1; REF-PROP02-GLOW@v1
- **acceptance checks**: Nothing in the hand moves for the full 3.0 s, thumb included: no flinch, squeeze, wrap or drift toward the phone. A full-palm wrap fails the take. The rings start only once the phone moves at 0.21 s and are flat by about 1.25 s, with no splash or wave, and at 100% on a 6-inch phone they visibly bend the reflected edge.; The rattle runs from 0.21 to 1.0 s and stays within 3 mm, and the phone settles back touching the mug. The wrist crosses the port end without touching the phone, the plug or the cable.; The card zone (bare oak, 54-64% height, 8-86% width) holds no object, hand, drip, cable or hot highlight for the whole shot.; The clean pass is shot at the same lock and exposure, with the phone lit face-down at its settled mark and nothing else changed. Its edges, reach and level match REF-PROP02-GLOW@v1.; Continuity: the hand is in the same still state at entry and exit. The dripper stays on the mark, front to the lens, spent filter in. One take with SH04. The mole is on the right hand only.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Practical, one take with SH04. Triage 7: liquids 2 (the rings are the beat), light switching on 2 (the glow, via SH05-COMP), screens 1 (face-down glass), reflections 1 (coffee surface), cloth 1 (cuff). The alternatives are already in use: practical rings and hand, composite glow. The rings must come from the phone, never from her grip, so the ring test has to pass first (blocker). If it fails, SH05 is re-boarded from C4' and this job is rewritten.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; shoot cost is the producer's

#### SH05-COMP · SH05

- **job type**: video
- **method**: Composite: PASS-SH05-GLOW@v1 layered onto PLT-SH05@v1 and tracked to the phone's rattle. Nothing is generated. Where the pass shows no light, none is added. Outputs COMP-SH05@v1.
- **prompt**: The face-down phone touching the mug lights at 0.21 seconds as it starts to rattle. Which edges leak, how far the cool light reaches onto the oak and how bright it is all match the clean glow pass at the plate's exposure. Light shows only where the pass shows it, and the gap holds only soft, even light. The glow moves with the phone through the rattle and is gone at 1.0 second as the rattle stops, leaving the phone dark and the hand as it was. Ambient kitchen room tone only.
- **reference asset ids**: PLT-SH05@v1; PASS-SH05-GLOW@v1; REF-PROP02-GLOW@v1
- **acceptance checks**: Edges, reach and level match PASS-SH05-GLOW@v1. No light is added where the pass shows none, and nothing on the screen is legible.; The glow starts with the rattle at 0.21 s, tracks the phone with no slip and is gone at 1.0 s. It stays out of the card zone (54-64% height) and off the mug, the hand and the dripper.; Every frame outside the glow matches the selected take exactly.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Post composite of a practical pass. A generated glow risks a legible screen or light on the wrong edge. If REF-PROP02-GLOW@v1 shows no leak, nothing is added. Triage as SH05-PLT.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; compositing time is the producer's

#### SH06-PLT · SH06

- **job type**: video
- **method**: Practical locked plate of the real unit: a same-axis 1.7x cut-in on SH05's line, 84f (3.5 s), with no retouch and nothing tracked on. Outputs PLT-SH06@v1. Cards are composited in the edit.
- **prompt**: Closer on the same line, the lens lowered to about standing eye height, window light from frame-left with the dripper's frame-left flank held a little down, soft even fill from the ceiling over the aisle. The empty dripper stands alone just right of centre, whole, about half the frame wide, the brightest shape on the oak. Its marked front, as in REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1, is square to the lens and carries the brightest highlight in frame. A soft, dark corner of the face-down phone holds at the frame-left edge, beside the bare oak where the mug stood. The bare oak below the dripper stays empty for type. Everything holds still for three and a half seconds, and the air over the rim is clear. The whole dripper is sharp from front rim to back rim, and the near oak is soft. Only the counter and what sits on it are in frame. Ambient kitchen room tone only.
- **reference asset ids**: REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1
- **acceptance checks**: Cut-in: the dripper spans at least 40% of frame width (47% planned), rim at 38-86% width. The phone's dark wedge sits at the frame-left edge (0-16% width), and the mug's footprint is bare oak with no ring. The front matches REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1:V2, square to the lens.; Swap-test read: the front's glaze arc is at least 40 px tall in the 1080-wide frame, checked at 100% on a 6-inch phone. If the signed-off unit has no pool, check the production designer's replacement swap-test detail at the same read. Failing that, the whole dripper must be legible at 40% of frame width or more under the 'Hand-glazed stoneware.' card.; Nothing moves across the 3.5 s. Glaze and foot are as shot, not retouched. The air over the rim is clear, there is no ring, stain or drip on the oak, the exterior is unmarked and no cable is in frame. The frame carries the 'Hand-glazed stoneware.' card, so the producer's claims check signs off the take.; The super zone (bare oak, 50-64% height, 8-84% width) holds no object, drip or hot highlight for all 3.5 s.; Continuity: the dripper is empty on the set-down mark, filter out, outer glaze dry. The phone is face-down, dark and unmoved, and the mug is gone.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Practical. Triage 2: reflections 2 (gloss at the foot, rim band). It stays practical because a generated 3.5 s hold drifts, and a generated pool would be false proof of 'Hand-glazed stoneware'. The 42 px read is a hypothesis to test before the shoot (blocker). If it reads under 40 px, the 1.15 m variant is used.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; shoot cost is the producer's

#### SH07-STL · SH07

- **job type**: image
- **method**: Practical still plate, 4:5, strobe-frozen (flash duration 1/2000 s or shorter), with the real unit and the hand double pouring left-handed. A separate plate, not a crop. Outputs STL-SH07@v1.
- **prompt**: Steep view down from behind and beside the right shoulder, window light from frame-left, soft fill from frame-right. At upper right the dripper sits on the white mug, whole and unblocked, its front to the lens. A thin stream from a plain terracotta kettle's spout is frozen as it lands on a dark domed bloom inside white paper. The kettle body, a dark scorch band round its base, sits at upper left. The left hand's fingers curl round its pale wooden handle in the top-left corner, the back of the index finger toward the lens and well inside the frame edges. The face-down phone lies dark below the spout, touching the mug. Bare oak fills the lower half for type. Stream, bloom, rim, scorch band and phone are sharp, the bottom oak soft. Only those fingers show of her.
- **reference asset ids**: REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1; CHR-NORA-HAND@v1
- **acceptance checks**: The stream is frozen as one thin thread landing on the dome, with no ghosting. The dripper is whole and unobstructed and matches REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1:V5, front to the lens. The copy zone (bare oak, 55-92% height, 8-92% width) is clear of every object.; The scorch band reads at 30-32% of frame height (S2's detail).; The scar reads on the back of the left index finger's middle joint, uncropped and at least 6% inside the left and top edges. A scar on the right hand means the frame is mirrored: reject. Only the left fingers show: no wrist, cuff, forearm, shoulder or hip, and the right hand is out. The frame top sits at least 5% below the window sill. Nails are short and bare.; Continuity: the phone lies face-down, dark and unplugged, touching the mug. The set-down mark is clean oak, and the cable is struck.; Bloom and steam stay as shot, with no retouch. The subline states verified facts, so the producer's claims check signs off the frame.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Practical still. Triage 3: liquids 2, reflections 1 (gloss rim, wet bloom). The kettle is a prop. The scar and sill locks wait on the C6 frame test (blocker): the plan's first estimate puts the scar joint at 2-4% width, which fails the 6% check. The film director's note said no back of the hand shows, but the board now locks the scar in frame, so the mirror check uses the scar.
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; shoot cost is the producer's

#### SH08-STL · SH08

- **job type**: image
- **method**: Practical still packshot, 4:5: the real unit alone on bare oak, neutral colour balance, no retouch. Outputs STL-SH08@v1.
- **prompt**: A low view, fifteen degrees down from about a metre away, of the empty dripper alone on bare oak. It stands centred in the lower half of the frame, a little under half the frame wide, its rim a shallow ellipse. Its marked front, as in REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1, is square to the lens and carries the brightest highlight. Window light from frame-left, the frame-left flank held a little down, neutral colour so the glaze reads true. The foot of a plain grey-green wall runs behind it, with the wall soft and empty above for type and a single course of pale tile crossing the very top of the frame. Sharp from rim to foot, near oak soft, bare oak on every side of the foot.
- **reference asset ids**: REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1
- **acceptance checks**: Silhouette, glaze and foot match REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1:V1 with no retouch, and colour matches V1 against the 18% card. The exterior is unmarked, and the front is square to the lens.; Swap-test read: the front's glaze pool is at least 40 px tall in the 1080-wide frame at 100% on a 6-inch phone. If the signed-off unit has no pool, check the production designer's replacement swap-test detail at the same read. Failing that, the whole dripper must be legible at 40% of frame width or more (44% planned).; The dripper is centred at 50% width and 50-82% height, with its foot on the set-down mark on clean oak and no ring. The wall copy zone (8-40% height) is clear, and the phone, cable, mug, kettle and window are out of frame.; The subline states verified facts (hand-glazed, dishwasher safe), so the producer's claims check signs off the frame.
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Practical packshot. Triage 2: reflections 2 (gloss at the foot, rim band). This is the DTC image, and a generator would add marks, invent the pool or shift the colour. The neutral balance departs from R1's warm light (ROUTE LIGHT, open with Lucas).
- **estimated cost unit**: 0 generation units; shoot cost is the producer's

## Integrity check

Recommendation: revise.

| Severity | Where | Issue | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| critical | SH02A-SH08 (D01 1.50-15.00 s; D02-1; D02-2) | world_bible PROP-01 says 'Shape and size are ASSUMED ... no dimensions, colour or finish were supplied'. The cream glaze, speck, rim band, the uneven foot pool and the raw foot band are all invented. The script uses the thumb crossing that pool as the in-use proof of the verified fact (B2 decision). SH06 and SH08 are lit around the pool's upper fillet, and the textless D02-2 doubles as the DTC product image. Generation plan: 'REF-PROP01-SAMPLE@v1 does not exist.' If the real unit differs, the spot and the DTC image show a feature or colour the product does not have, which is a visual claim outside the verified facts. Fix: get a brand-confirmed production sample and views V1-V8 before any product plate. Then re-pick the B2/B5/B6/D02-2 mark if needed, re-run the contrast checks, and re-plan the SH02B seat and the casting rim-diameter check. | producer |
| major | SH02A-SH05 (D01 1.88-10.00 s; f45-f240) | The sound plan was timed to superseded shot starts. The current starts are SH02A f36, SH02B f60, SH03 f108, SH04 f168 and SH05 f204. Mismatches: (1) rack knock f45-48 against a lift at f50-f58; (2) seat click f67 against a picture click at f74; (3) kettle tip f113 and stream landing f115 against a spout tip at f118; (4) last drip f189 against f172 (17f late); (5) set-down f203 against about f188 (15f late); (6) the B4b pulse at f221-f240 against a picture rattle at f209-f228. So the buzz starts about 0.5 s after the rings and runs 12f past the script's 9.5 s card-in. The sound plan's own label reads 'SH05 5f-24f' but places the pulse at f221. The storyboard's SYNC flag confirms all six. Fix: move each hit to its picture frame and re-derive the SND-D01-001 tone windows and loudest/quietest moments. The storyboard then re-boards the cue lines. | sound_designer |
| major | SH01, SH02A, SH02B, SH03, SH04, SH05, SH07; KIT-NORA-HAND | The hand double is a real person whose hands carry NORA in paid and organic social and on the DTC page. The release is 'pending, not cleared' in casting blocker 2, treatment blocker 7, camera blocker 1, storyboard blocker 4 and generation blocker 2, and the left-handed pour test has not run. Question for Lucas: approve the scope (D01 9:16, D02 stills, DTC site, territory, term) and the fee, and sign the release before the fitting. No hand plate may be shot until it is signed. | producer |
| major | D01 SH01-SH06 (0.00-15.00 s) | Route R1 visual_language asks for a 'handheld-but-steady camera, close but not macro'. Camera plan: 'Every shot in the plan is locked (route departure, blocker 5).' The departure is not approved. The '7:00' composite, the SH04-SH05 single take and both same-axis cut-ins depend on the lock. Question for Lucas: approve the locked grammar, or approve handheld for S1 only at the cost of a tracked composite. | producer |
| major | B2 cards 1.50-4.50 s (SH02A/SH02B); B4b 9.50-11.50 s (SH05); B5-B6 11.50-15.00 s (SH06) | The treatment's blocking puts every card 'in the world bible's bare card area (wall-D paint, 125 cm up and above)', including S4 ('Cards and the sign-off sit in the world bible's bare card area'). Camera plan blocker 3 says that area 'falls at 0-11% h, above the safe band' in SH02B and 'is above the frame in SH02A and SH04-SH06', and SH06 notes 'The wall-D card area is above the frame'. The camera plan's substitute B2 zone (16-26% h) sits over TILE-01's course and bullnose. The treatment's placement cannot be executed, and the camera plan's zones are not approved. Fix: the director approves the screen zones and corrects the treatment, and the storyboard checks legibility over the tile band. | director |
| major | SH05 (D01-B4b 8.50-11.50 s) | The sound-off story turn depends on the phone's buzz rippling the coffee through the mug so the rings visibly bend TILE-01's reflected edge. The treatment lists this as hypothesis (2), untested. In the 43 cm frame the coffee is about 1.2 × 0.7 cm on a 6-inch screen, and the fingertip contact may damp the vibration. Fallbacks exist (a 30 cm reframe from C4', a concealed coin motor, holding on the still hand), but the test has not been run or scheduled. Fix: run the prelight ring test (treatment blocker 2) on the built set with the hand double's grip and publish the result before the shoot. | production_designer |
| major | SH07 (D02-1) | Camera plan: 'The plan's first estimate misses both: the joint lands at about 2-4% w, 8-12% h, and the sill sits close to the top-left corner.' The locks require at least 6% inside the frame edge for the scar and at least 5% below the WIN-01 sill. The SH07-STL acceptance check would reject the planned frame, and a visible window breaks the taste notes. Fix: rework C6's aim or lens, or the handle position, and prove it in the C6 frame test before the shoot. The scar must never be cropped. | cinematographer |
| major | Whole package (production method and budget) | Generation plan: 'No job needs it, because all nine panels ... name a practical or composite path', at 0 generation units. The world bible specifies a studio build (counter run, backsplash, full-height flats on walls A, B and C, CEIL-01), and the plan also needs a hand double, a foley session in a real kitchen and prelight glow, ring, bloom and drip tests. Every job reads 'shoot cost is the producer's'. No budget, schedule, crew or approval is recorded.
